<task>
Act as the implementation owner for one dependency-ordered semantic-template repair phase. Complete only phase attention_rope_operators: Implement the immutable target-model Q/K/V projection, captured-position RoPE, causal GQA QK-softmax-PV, and output projection semantics. Reuse the supplied compile-validated Linear and Softmax dependency interfaces rather than duplicating their arithmetic. Consume the supplied runtime-constant stream contract exactly; do not calculate a different model-family convention in a wrapper. Return complete compile-ready file contents for this phase; do not wait for later phases and do not claim any verification gate has passed.
</task>

<rules>
1. Return status=ready_to_apply when this phase is complete and compile-ready even though later semantic phases remain; use blocked only for exact facts missing from this phase bundle.
2. Edit only exact Scala paths listed in semantic_template_repair_phase_bundle.editable_contract.approved_bounded_template_repair_exact_files.
3. Treat phase.dependency_template_files and semantic_template_repair_phase_bundle.read_only_dependency_template_files as exact read-only interfaces; consume them but never return them in file_edits.
4. Replace every file named in phase.required_template_files in both the persistent framework-template root and current generated-template root. The paired contents must be byte-identical and use the exact supplied pre-edit SHA-256 values.
5. Provide complete file contents, never snippets, ellipses, prose patches, behavioral wrappers, simulator-only arithmetic, or human follow-up instructions.
6. Use the hash-verified target-model implementation, semantic adapter, stage numeric/weight-layout contracts, and trusted HardFloat/QuantCommon support as the only semantic and arithmetic authorities.
7. Preserve external accelerator/board ABI, stage order, dimensions, stream ordering, memory/runtime contracts, numeric policy, checkpoint/input/reference/checker hashes, and all non-phase files.
8. This phase is implementation-in-progress. Do not create a DUT binding manifest or semantic harness, edit emitted SystemVerilog, alter expected outputs/checkers, or claim operator/layer correctness.
9. Request only sbt --no-server Compile/compile in the exact generated Chisel project. The executor runs a mandatory compile even if the request is omitted.
</rules>

\ud83d\udea8\ud83d\udea8\ud83d\udea8\ud83d\udea8\ud83d\udea8\ud83d\udea8\ud83d\udea8\ud83d\udea8\ud83d\udea8\ud83d\udea8\ud83d\udea8\ud83d\udea8\ud83d\udea8\ud83d\udea8\ud83d\udea8\ud83d\udea8\ud83d\udea8\ud83d\udea8\ud83d\udea8\ud83d\udea8\ud83d\udea8\ud83d\udea8\ud83d\udea8\ud83d\udea8\ud83d\udea8\ud83d\udea8\ud83d\udea8\ud83d\udea8\ud83d\udea8\ud83d\udea8\ud83d\udea8\ud83d\udea8\ud83d\udea8\ud83d\udea8\ud83d\udea8\ud83d\udea8\ud83d\udea8\ud83d\udea8\ud83d\udea8\ud83d\udea8\ud83d\udea8\ud83d\udea8\ud83d\udea8\ud83d\udea8\ud83d\udea8\ud83d\udea8\ud83d\udea8\ud83d\udea8\nfrom typing import Callable, Optional, Union\n\nimport torch\nfrom torch import nn\n\nfrom ...activations import ACT2FN\nfrom ...cache_utils import Cache, DynamicCache\nfrom ...generation import GenerationMixin\nfrom ...integrations import use_kernel_forward_from_hub\nfrom ...masking_utils import create_causal_mask, create_sliding_window_causal_mask\nfrom ...modeling_flash_attention_utils import FlashAttentionKwargs\nfrom ...modeling_layers import (\n    GenericForQuestionAnswering,\n    GenericForSequenceClassification,\n    GenericForTokenClassification,\n    GradientCheckpointingLayer,\n)\nfrom ...modeling_outputs import BaseModelOutputWithPast, CausalLMOutputWithPast\nfrom ...modeling_rope_utils import ROPE_INIT_FUNCTIONS, dynamic_rope_update\nfrom ...modeling_utils import ALL_ATTENTION_FUNCTIONS, PreTrainedModel\nfrom ...processing_utils import Unpack\nfrom ...utils import TransformersKwargs, auto_docstring, can_return_tuple\nfrom ...utils.deprecation import deprecate_kwarg\nfrom ...utils.generic import check_model_inputs\nfrom .configuration_qwen2 import Qwen2Config\n\n\nclass Qwen2MLP(nn.Module):\n    def __init__(self, config):\n        super().__init__()\n        self.config = config\n        self.hidden_size = config.hidden_size\n        self.intermediate_size = config.intermediate_size\n        self.gate_proj = nn.Linear(self.hidden_size, self.intermediate_size, bias=False)\n        self.up_proj = nn.Linear(self.hidden_size, self.intermediate_size, bias=False)\n        self.down_proj = nn.Linear(self.intermediate_size, self.hidden_size, bias=False)\n        self.act_fn = ACT2FN[config.hidden_act]\n\n    def forward(self, x):\n        down_proj = self.down_proj(self.act_fn(self.gate_proj(x)) * self.up_proj(x))\n        return down_proj\n\n\ndef rotate_half(x):\n    \"\"\"Rotates half the hidden dims of the input.\"\"\"\n    x1 = x[..., : x.shape[-1] // 2]\n    x2 = x[..., x.shape[-1] // 2 :]\n    return torch.cat((-x2, x1), dim=-1)\n\n\ndef apply_rotary_pos_emb(q, k, cos, sin, position_ids=None, unsqueeze_dim=1):\n    \"\"\"Applies Rotary Position Embedding to the query and key tensors.\n\n    Args:\n        q (`torch.Tensor`): The query tensor.\n        k (`torch.Tensor`): The key tensor.\n        cos (`torch.Tensor`): The cosine part of the rotary embedding.\n        sin (`torch.Tensor`): The sine part of the rotary embedding.\n        position_ids (`torch.Tensor`, *optional*):\n            Deprecated and unused.\n        unsqueeze_dim (`int`, *optional*, defaults to 1):\n            The 'unsqueeze_dim' argument specifies the dimension along which to unsqueeze cos[position_ids] and\n            sin[position_ids] so that they can be properly broadcasted to the dimensions of q and k. For example, note\n            that cos[position_ids] and sin[position_ids] have the shape [batch_size, seq_len, head_dim]. Then, if q and\n            k have the shape [batch_size, heads, seq_len, head_dim], then setting unsqueeze_dim=1 makes\n            cos[position_ids] and sin[position_ids] broadcastable to the shapes of q and k. Similarly, if q and k have\n            the shape [batch_size, seq_len, heads, head_dim], then set unsqueeze_dim=2.\n    Returns:\n        `tuple(torch.Tensor)` comprising of the query and key tensors rotated using the Rotary Position Embedding.\n    \"\"\"\n    cos = cos.unsqueeze(unsqueeze_dim)\n    sin = sin.unsqueeze(unsqueeze_dim)\n    q_embed = (q * cos) + (rotate_half(q) * sin)\n    k_embed = (k * cos) + (rotate_half(k) * sin)\n    return q_embed, k_embed\n\n\ndef repeat_kv(hidden_states: torch.Tensor, n_rep: int) -> torch.Tensor:\n    \"\"\"\n    This is the equivalent of torch.repeat_interleave(x, dim=1, repeats=n_rep). The hidden states go from (batch,\n    num_key_value_heads, seqlen, head_dim) to (batch, num_attention_heads, seqlen, head_dim)\n    \"\"\"\n    batch, num_key_value_heads, slen, head_dim = hidden_states.shape\n    if n_rep == 1:\n        return hidden_states\n    hidden_states = hidden_states[:, :, None, :, :].expand(batch, num_key_value_heads, n_rep, slen, head_dim)\n    return hidden_states.reshape(batch, num_key_value_heads * n_rep, slen, head_dim)\n\n\ndef eager_attention_forward(\n    module: nn.Module,\n    query: torch.Tensor,\n    key: torch.Tensor,\n    value: torch.Tensor,\n    attention_mask: Optional[torch.Tensor],\n    scaling: float,\n    dropout: float = 0.0,\n    **kwargs: Unpack[TransformersKwargs],\n):\n    key_states = repeat_kv(key, module.num_key_value_groups)\n    value_states = repeat_kv(value, module.num_key_value_groups)\n\n    attn_weights = torch.matmul(query, key_states.transpose(2, 3)) * scaling\n    if attention_mask is not None:\n        causal_mask = attention_mask[:, :, :, : key_states.shape[-2]]\n        attn_weights = attn_weights + causal_mask\n\n    attn_weights = nn.functional.softmax(attn_weights, dim=-1, dtype=torch.float32).to(query.dtype)\n    attn_weights = nn.functional.dropout(attn_weights, p=dropout, training=module.training)\n    attn_output = torch.matmul(attn_weights, value_states)\n    attn_output = attn_output.transpose(1, 2).contiguous()\n\n    return attn_output, attn_weights\n\n\nclass Qwen2Attention(nn.Module):\n    \"\"\"Multi-headed attention from 'Attention Is All You Need' paper\"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, config: Qwen2Config, layer_idx: int):\n        super().__init__()\n        self.config = config\n        self.layer_idx = layer_idx\n        self.head_dim = getattr(config, \"head_dim\", config.hidden_size // config.num_attention_heads)\n        self.num_key_value_groups = config.num_attention_heads // config.num_key_value_heads\n        self.scaling = self.head_dim**-0.5\n        self.attention_dropout = config.attention_dropout\n        self.is_causal = True\n        self.q_proj = nn.Linear(config.hidden_size, config.num_attention_heads * self.head_dim, bias=True)\n        self.k_proj = nn.Linear(config.hidden_size, config.num_key_value_heads * self.head_dim, bias=True)\n        self.v_proj = nn.Linear(config.hidden_size, config.num_key_value_heads * self.head_dim, bias=True)\n        self.o_proj = nn.Linear(config.num_attention_heads * self.head_dim, config.hidden_size, bias=False)\n        self.sliding_window = config.sliding_window if config.layer_types[layer_idx] == \"sliding_attention\" else None\n\n    @deprecate_kwarg(\"past_key_value\", new_name=\"past_key_values\", version=\"4.58\")\n    def forward(\n        self,\n        hidden_states: torch.Tensor,\n        position_embeddings: tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor],\n        attention_mask: Optional[torch.Tensor],\n        past_key_values: Optional[Cache] = None,\n        cache_position: Optional[torch.LongTensor] = None,\n        **kwargs: Unpack[FlashAttentionKwargs],\n    ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, Optional[torch.Tensor]]:\n        input_shape = hidden_states.shape[:-1]\n        hidden_shape = (*input_shape, -1, self.head_dim)\n\n        query_states = self.q_proj(hidden_states).view(hidden_shape).transpose(1, 2)\n        key_states = self.k_proj(hidden_states).view(hidden_shape).transpose(1, 2)\n        value_states = self.v_proj(hidden_states).view(hidden_shape).transpose(1, 2)\n\n        cos, sin = position_embeddings\n        query_states, key_states = apply_rotary_pos_emb(query_states, key_states, cos, sin)\n\n        if past_key_values is not None:\n            # sin and cos are specific to RoPE models; cache_position needed for the static cache\n            cache_kwargs = {\"sin\": sin, \"cos\": cos, \"cache_position\": cache_position}\n            key_states, value_states = past_key_values.update(key_states, value_states, self.layer_idx, cache_kwargs)\n\n        attention_interface: Callable = eager_attention_forward\n        if self.config._attn_implementation != \"eager\":\n            attention_interface = ALL_ATTENTION_FUNCTIONS[self.config._attn_implementation]\n\n        attn_output, attn_weights = attention_interface(\n            self,\n            query_states,\n            key_states,\n            value_states,\n            attention_mask,\n            dropout=0.0 if not self.training else self.attention_dropout,\n            scaling=self.scaling,\n            sliding_window=self.sliding_window,  # main diff with Llama\n            **kwargs,\n        )\n\n        attn_output = attn_output.reshape(*input_shape, -1).contiguous()\n        attn_output = self.o_proj(attn_output)\n        return attn_output, attn_weights\n\n\n@use_kernel_forward_from_hub(\"RMSNorm\")\nclass Qwen2RMSNorm(nn.Module):\n    def __init__(self, hidden_size, eps: float = 1e-6) -> None:\n        \"\"\"\n        Qwen2RMSNorm is equivalent to T5LayerNorm\n        \"\"\"\n        super().__init__()\n        self.weight = nn.Parameter(torch.ones(hidden_size))\n        self.variance_epsilon = eps\n\n    def forward(self, hidden_states: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:\n        input_dtype = hidden_states.dtype\n        hidden_states = hidden_states.to(torch.float32)\n        variance = hidden_states.pow(2).mean(-1, keepdim=True)\n        hidden_states = hidden_states * torch.rsqrt(variance + self.variance_epsilon)\n        return self.weight * hidden_states.to(input_dtype)\n\n    def extra_repr(self):\n        return f\"{tuple(self.weight.shape)}, eps={self.variance_epsilon}\"\n\n\nclass Qwen2DecoderLayer(GradientCheckpointingLayer):\n    def __init__(self, config: Qwen2Config, layer_idx: int):\n        super().__init__()\n        self.hidden_size = config.hidden_size\n\n        self.self_attn = Qwen2Attention(config=config, layer_idx=layer_idx)\n\n        self.mlp = Qwen2MLP(config)\n        self.input_layernorm = Qwen2RMSNorm(config.hidden_size, eps=config.rms_norm_eps)\n        self.post_attention_layernorm = Qwen2RMSNorm(config.hidden_size, eps=config.rms_norm_eps)\n        self.attention_type = config.layer_types[layer_idx]\n\n    @deprecate_kwarg(\"past_key_value\", new_name=\"past_key_values\", version=\"4.58\")\n    def forward(\n        self,\n        hidden_states: torch.Tensor,\n        attention_mask: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None,\n        position_ids: Optional[torch.LongTensor] = None,\n        past_key_values: Optional[Cache] = None,\n        use_cache: Optional[bool] = False,\n        cache_position: Optional[torch.LongTensor] = None,\n        position_embeddings: Optional[tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]] = None,  # necessary, but kept here for BC\n        **kwargs: Unpack[TransformersKwargs],\n    ) -> torch.Tensor:\n        residual = hidden_states\n        hidden_states = self.input_layernorm(hidden_states)\n        # Self Attention\n        hidden_states, _ = self.self_attn(\n            hidden_states=hidden_states,\n            attention_mask=attention_mask,\n            position_ids=position_ids,\n            past_key_values=past_key_values,\n            use_cache=use_cache,\n            cache_position=cache_position,\n            position_embeddings=position_embeddings,\n            **kwargs,\n        )\n        hidden_states = residual + hidden_states\n\n        # Fully Connected\n        residual = hidden_states\n        hidden_states = self.post_attention_layernorm(hidden_states)\n        hidden_states = self.mlp(hidden_states)\n        hidden_states = residual + hidden_states\n        return hidden_states\n\n\n@auto_docstring\nclass Qwen2PreTrainedModel(PreTrainedModel):\n    config: Qwen2Config\n    base_model_prefix = \"model\"\n    supports_gradient_checkpointing = True\n    _no_split_modules = [\"Qwen2DecoderLayer\"]\n    _skip_keys_device_placement = [\"past_key_values\"]\n    _supports_flash_attn = True\n    _supports_sdpa = True\n    _supports_flex_attn = True\n\n    _can_compile_fullgraph = True\n    _supports_attention_backend = True\n    _can_record_outputs = {\n        \"hidden_states\": Qwen2DecoderLayer,\n        \"attentions\": Qwen2Attention,\n    }\n\n\nclass Qwen2RotaryEmbedding(nn.Module):\n    inv_freq: torch.Tensor  # fix linting for `register_buffer`\n\n    def __init__(self, config: Qwen2Config, device=None):\n        super().__init__()\n        # BC: \"rope_type\" was originally \"type\"\n        if hasattr(config, \"rope_scaling\") and isinstance(config.rope_scaling, dict):\n            self.rope_type = config.rope_scaling.get(\"rope_type\", config.rope_scaling.get(\"type\"))\n        else:\n            self.rope_type = \"default\"\n        self.max_seq_len_cached = config.max_position_embeddings\n        self.original_max_seq_len = config.max_position_embeddings\n\n        self.config = config\n        self.rope_init_fn = ROPE_INIT_FUNCTIONS[self.rope_type]\n\n        inv_freq, self.attention_scaling = self.rope_init_fn(self.config, device)\n        self.register_buffer(\"inv_freq\", inv_freq, persistent=False)\n        self.original_inv_freq = self.inv_freq\n\n    @torch.no_grad()\n    @dynamic_rope_update  # power user: used with advanced RoPE types (e.g. dynamic rope)\n    def forward(self, x, position_ids):\n        inv_freq_expanded = self.inv_freq[None, :, None].float().expand(position_ids.shape[0], -1, 1).to(x.device)\n        position_ids_expanded = position_ids[:, None, :].float()\n\n        device_type = x.device.type if isinstance(x.device.type, str) and x.device.type != \"mps\" else \"cpu\"\n        with torch.autocast(device_type=device_type, enabled=False):  # Force float32\n            freqs = (inv_freq_expanded.float() @ position_ids_expanded.float()).transpose(1, 2)\n            emb = torch.cat((freqs, freqs), dim=-1)\n            cos = emb.cos() * self.attention_scaling\n            sin = emb.sin() * self.attention_scaling\n\n        return cos.to(dtype=x.dtype), sin.to(dtype=x.dtype)\n\n\n@auto_docstring\nclass Qwen2Model(Qwen2PreTrainedModel):\n    def __init__(self, config: Qwen2Config):\n        super().__init__(config)\n        self.padding_idx = config.pad_token_id\n        self.vocab_size = config.vocab_size\n\n        self.embed_tokens = nn.Embedding(config.vocab_size, config.hidden_size, self.padding_idx)\n        self.layers = nn.ModuleList(\n            [Qwen2DecoderLayer(config, layer_idx) for layer_idx in range(config.num_hidden_layers)]\n        )\n        self.norm = Qwen2RMSNorm(config.hidden_size, eps=config.rms_norm_eps)\n        self.rotary_emb = Qwen2RotaryEmbedding(config=config)\n        self.gradient_checkpointing = False\n        self.has_sliding_layers = \"sliding_attention\" in self.config.layer_types\n\n        # Initialize weights and apply final processing\n        self.post_init()\n\n    @check_model_inputs\n    @auto_docstring\n    def forward(\n        self,\n        input_ids: Optional[torch.LongTensor] = None,\n        attention_mask: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None,\n        position_ids: Optional[torch.LongTensor] = None,\n        past_key_values: Optional[Cache] = None,\n        inputs_embeds: Optional[torch.FloatTensor] = None,\n        use_cache: Optional[bool] = None,\n        cache_position: Optional[torch.LongTensor] = None,\n        **kwargs: Unpack[TransformersKwargs],\n    ) -> BaseModelOutputWithPast:\n        if (input_ids is None) ^ (inputs_embeds is not None):\n            raise ValueError(\"You must specify exactly one of input_ids or inputs_embeds\")\n\n        if inputs_embeds is None:\n            inputs_embeds = self.embed_tokens(input_ids)\n\n        if use_cache and past_key_values is None:\n            past_key_values = DynamicCache(config=self.config)\n\n        if cache_position is None:\n            past_seen_tokens = past_key_values.get_seq_length() if past_key_values is not None else 0\n            cache_position = torch.arange(\n                past_seen_tokens, past_seen_tokens + inputs_embeds.shape[1], device=inputs_embeds.device\n            )\n\n        if position_ids is None:\n            position_ids = cache_position.unsqueeze(0)\n\n        # It may already have been prepared by e.g. `generate`\n        if not isinstance(causal_mask_mapping := attention_mask, dict):\n            # Prepare mask arguments\n            mask_kwargs = {\n                \"config\": self.config,\n                \"input_embeds\": inputs_embeds,\n                \"attention_mask\": attention_mask,\n                \"cache_position\": cache_position,\n                \"past_key_values\": past_key_values,\n                \"position_ids\": position_ids,\n            }\n            # Create the masks\n            causal_mask_mapping = {\n                \"full_attention\": create_causal_mask(**mask_kwargs),\n            }\n            # The sliding window alternating layers are not always activated depending on the config\n            if self.has_sliding_layers:\n                causal_mask_mapping[\"sliding_attention\"] = create_sliding_window_causal_mask(**mask_kwargs)\n\n        hidden_states = inputs_embeds\n\n        # create position embeddings to be shared across the decoder layers\n        position_embeddings = self.rotary_emb(hidden_states, position_ids)\n\n        for decoder_layer in self.layers[: self.config.num_hidden_layers]:\n            hidden_states = decoder_layer(\n                hidden_states,\n                attention_mask=causal_mask_mapping[decoder_layer.attention_type],\n                position_ids=position_ids,\n                past_key_values=past_key_values,\n                use_cache=use_cache,\n                cache_position=cache_position,\n                position_embeddings=position_embeddings,\n                **kwargs,\n            )\n\n        hidden_states = self.norm(hidden_states)\n        return BaseModelOutputWithPast(\n            last_hidden_state=hidden_states,\n            past_key_values=past_key_values if use_cache else None,\n        )\n\n\n@auto_docstring\nclass Qwen2ForCausalLM(Qwen2PreTrainedModel, GenerationMixin):\n    _tied_weights_keys = [\"lm_head.weight\"]\n    _tp_plan = {\"lm_head\": \"colwise_rep\"}\n    _pp_plan = {\"lm_head\": ([\"hidden_states\"], [\"logits\"])}\n\n    def __init__(self, config):\n        super().__init__(config)\n        self.model = Qwen2Model(config)\n        self.vocab_size = config.vocab_size\n        self.lm_head = nn.Linear(config.hidden_size, config.vocab_size, bias=False)\n\n        # Initialize weights and apply final processing\n        self.post_init()\n\n    @can_return_tuple\n    @auto_docstring\n    def forward(\n        self,\n        input_ids: Optional[torch.LongTensor] = None,\n        attention_mask: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None,\n        position_ids: Optional[torch.LongTensor] = None,\n        past_key_values: Optional[Cache] = None,\n        inputs_embeds: Optional[torch.FloatTensor] = None,\n        labels: Optional[torch.LongTensor] = None,\n        use_cache: Optional[bool] = None,\n        cache_position: Optional[torch.LongTensor] = None,\n        logits_to_keep: Union[int, torch.Tensor] = 0,\n        **kwargs: Unpack[TransformersKwargs],\n    ) -> CausalLMOutputWithPast:\n        r\"\"\"\n        Example:\n\n        ```python\n        >>> from transformers import AutoTokenizer, Qwen2ForCausalLM\n\n        >>> model = Qwen2ForCausalLM.from_pretrained(\"meta-qwen2/Qwen2-2-7b-hf\")\n        >>> tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(\"meta-qwen2/Qwen2-2-7b-hf\")\n\n        >>> prompt = \"Hey, are you conscious? Can you talk to me?\"\n        >>> inputs = tokenizer(prompt, return_tensors=\"pt\")\n\n        >>> # Generate\n        >>> generate_ids = model.generate(inputs.input_ids, max_length=30)\n        >>> tokenizer.batch_decode(generate_ids, skip_special_tokens=True, clean_up_tokenization_spaces=False)[0]\n        \"Hey, are you conscious? Can you talk to me?\\nI'm not conscious, but I can talk to you.\"\n        ```\"\"\"\n        outputs: BaseModelOutputWithPast = self.model(\n            input_ids=input_ids,\n            attention_mask=attention_mask,\n            position_ids=position_ids,\n            past_key_values=past_key_values,\n            inputs_embeds=inputs_embeds,\n            use_cache=use_cache,\n            cache_position=cache_position,\n            **kwargs,\n        )\n\n        hidden_states = outputs.last_hidden_state\n        # Only compute necessary logits, and do not upcast them to float if we are not computing the loss\n        slice_indices = slice(-logits_to_keep, None) if isinstance(logits_to_keep, int) else logits_to_keep\n        logits = self.lm_head(hidden_states[:, slice_indices, :])\n\n        loss = None\n        if labels is not None:\n            loss = self.loss_function(logits=logits, labels=labels, vocab_size=self.config.vocab_size, **kwargs)\n\n        return CausalLMOutputWithPast(\n            loss=loss,\n            logits=logits,\n            past_key_values=outputs.past_key_values,\n            hidden_states=outputs.hidden_states,\n            attentions=outputs.attentions,\n        )\n\n\nclass Qwen2ForSequenceClassification(GenericForSequenceClassification, Qwen2PreTrainedModel):\n    pass\n\n\nclass Qwen2ForTokenClassification(GenericForTokenClassification, Qwen2PreTrainedModel):\n    pass\n\n\nclass Qwen2ForQuestionAnswering(GenericForQuestionAnswering, Qwen2PreTrainedModel):\n    base_model_prefix = \"transformer\"  # For BC, where `transformer` was used instead of `model`\n\n\n__all__ = [\n    \"Qwen2PreTrainedModel\",\n    \"Qwen2Model\",\n    \"Qwen2ForCausalLM\",\n    \"Qwen2RMSNorm\",\n    \"Qwen2ForSequenceClassification\",\n    \"Qwen2ForTokenClassification\",\n    \"Qwen2ForQuestionAnswering\",\n]\n",

      "bytes": 496,
      "content": "ThisBuild / scalaVersion := \"2.13.16\"\nThisBuild / version := \"0.1.0\"\n\nval chiselVersion = \"7.0.0\"\n\nlazy val root = (project in file(\".\"))\n  .settings(\n    name := \"spatialaccagent-generated\",\n    libraryDependencies += \"org.chipsalliance\" %% \"chisel\" % chiselVersion,\n    scalacOptions ++= Seq(\"-language:reflectiveCalls\", \"-deprecation\", \"-feature\", \"-Xcheckinit\", \"-Ymacro-annotations\"),\n    addCompilerPlugin(\"org.chipsalliance\" % \"chisel-plugin\" % chiselVersion cross CrossVersion.full),\n  )\n",

      "content": "sbt.version = 1.9.7\n",

      "bytes": 2588,
      "content": "package spatialaccagent.templates\n\nimport chisel3._\nimport chisel3.util._\n\nfinal case class AttentionParams(\n  hiddenSize: Int,\n  qHeads: Int,\n  kvHeads: Int,\n  headDim: Int,\n  seqLen: Int,\n  lanes: Int = 12,\n  elemBits: Int = 8,\n  qkvElemsPerBeat: Int = 2,\n  batchSize: Int = 16\n) {\n  val qkv = QKVProjectionParams(hiddenSize, qHeads, kvHeads, headDim, lanes = lanes, elemBits = elemBits, qkvElemsPerBeat = qkvElemsPerBeat, batchSize = batchSize, maxSeqLen = seqLen)\n  val headVectorBits: Int = headDim * elemBits\n  val addrBits: Int = log2Ceil(qHeads max 2)\n}\n\nclass Attention(p: AttentionParams) extends Module {\n  val io = IO(new Bundle {\n    val cfg = Input(new StageConfig(log2Ceil(p.seqLen + 1 max 2)))\n    val cfgValid = Input(Bool())\n    val in = Flipped(Decoupled(new QKVStreamBeat(p.qkv)))\n    val mask = Input(UInt(p.seqLen.W))\n    val scoreScale = Input(UInt(32.W))\n    val ctxInvScale = Input(UInt(32.W))\n    val ctxZeroPoint = Input(UInt(8.W))\n    val outInvScale = Input(UInt(32.W))\n    val out = Decoupled(new StreamBeat(StreamSpec(p.headVectorBits, p.addrBits)))\n  })\n\n  // The current attention shell keeps the QKV stream contract. Softmax.scala is\n  // the reusable row-level template for the later QK -> softmax -> PV split.\n  val vVec = Reg(Vec(p.headDim, UInt(p.elemBits.W)))\n  val outReg = Reg(UInt(p.headVectorBits.W))\n  val outHead = Reg(UInt(p.addrBits.W))\n  val outLast = RegInit(false.B)\n  val outValid = RegInit(false.B)\n\n  val beatCnt = RegInit(0.U(log2Ceil(p.qkv.headBeats max 2).W))\n  val qkvFields = io.in.bits.data.asTypeOf(Vec(3 * p.qkvElemsPerBeat, UInt(p.elemBits.W)))\n  val nextV = WireDefault(vVec)\n  for (i <- 0 until p.qkvElemsPerBeat) {\n    val elemIdx = (beatCnt * p.qkvElemsPerBeat.U + i.U)(log2Ceil(p.headDim max 2) - 1, 0)\n    when(io.in.fire) {\n      nextV(elemIdx) := qkvFields(2 * p.qkvElemsPerBeat + i)\n    }\n  }\n\n  io.in.ready := !outValid || io.out.ready\n  when(io.in.fire) {\n    vVec := nextV\n    beatCnt := Mux(beatCnt === (p.qkv.headBeats - 1).U, 0.U, beatCnt + 1.U)\n    when(beatCnt === (p.qkv.headBeats - 1).U) {\n      outReg := nextV.asUInt\n      outHead := io.in.bits.head\n      outLast := io.in.bits.last\n      outValid := true.B\n    }\n  }\n\n  when(io.out.fire) {\n    outValid := false.B\n  }\n\n  io.out.valid := outValid\n  io.out.bits.data := outReg\n  io.out.bits.st := outHead === 0.U\n  io.out.bits.addr := outHead\n  io.out.bits.last := outLast\n}\n\nclass AttentionMHA(p: AttentionParams) extends Attention(p)\nclass AttentionGQA(p: AttentionParams) extends Attention(p)\nclass AttentionMQA(p: AttentionParams) extends Attention(p)\n",
      "path": "accagent/runs/spatialacc_qwen_agent_fast_run/generated/chisel/src/main/scala/spatialaccagent/templates/Attention.scala",
      "sha256": "d94b29bbf3329e108ccebacf6f3afcabb2d582419981187352fb7b9123dd6b15",
      "truncated": false
    },
    {
      "bytes": 4484,
      "content": "package spatialaccagent.templates\n\nimport chisel3._\nimport chisel3.util._\nimport hardfloat._\nimport hardfloat.consts\n\nobject AccMath {\n  def ceilDiv(a: Int, b: Int): Int = (a + b - 1) / b\n\n  def resizeSigned(x: SInt, width: Int): SInt = resizeSignedToUInt(x, width).asSInt\n\n  def resizeSignedToUInt(x: SInt, width: Int): UInt = {\n    val u = x.asUInt\n    if (width <= x.getWidth) {\n      u(width - 1, 0)\n    } else {\n      Cat(Fill(width - x.getWidth, u(x.getWidth - 1)), u)\n    }\n  }\n}\n\nobject IeeeMath {\n  private val fp16ExpWidth = 5\n  private val fp16SigWidth = 11\n  private val fp32ExpWidth = 8\n  private val fp32SigWidth = 24\n\n  val fp32Zero: UInt = 0.U(32.W)\n  val fp32One: UInt = \"h3f800000\".U(32.W)\n\n  def fp32Constant(value: Double): UInt = {\n    val bits = java.lang.Float.floatToRawIntBits(value.toFloat)\n    BigInt(bits.toLong & 0xffffffffL).U(32.W)\n  }\n\n  def toFp32(value: UInt, bits: Int): UInt = {\n    require(bits == 16 || bits == 32, s\"IEEE input width must be 16 or 32, got $bits\")\n    if (bits == 32) {\n      value\n    } else {\n      val convert = Module(new RecFNToRecFN(fp16ExpWidth, fp16SigWidth, fp32ExpWidth, fp32SigWidth))\n      convert.io.in := recFNFromFN(fp16ExpWidth, fp16SigWidth, value)\n      convert.io.roundingMode := consts.round_near_even\n      convert.io.detectTininess := consts.tininess_afterRounding\n      fNFromRecFN(fp32ExpWidth, fp32SigWidth, convert.io.out)\n    }\n  }\n\n  def fromFp32(value: UInt, bits: Int): UInt = {\n    require(bits == 16 || bits == 32, s\"IEEE output width must be 16 or 32, got $bits\")\n    if (bits == 32) {\n      value\n    } else {\n      val convert = Module(new RecFNToRecFN(fp32ExpWidth, fp32SigWidth, fp16ExpWidth, fp16SigWidth))\n      convert.io.in := recFNFromFN(fp32ExpWidth, fp32SigWidth, value)\n      convert.io.roundingMode := consts.round_near_even\n      convert.io.detectTininess := consts.tininess_afterRounding\n      fNFromRecFN(fp16ExpWidth, fp16SigWidth, convert.io.out)\n    }\n  }\n\n  def addFp32(a: UInt, b: UInt): UInt = {\n    val add = Module(new AddRecFN(fp32ExpWidth, fp32SigWidth))\n    add.io.subOp := false.B\n    add.io.a := recFNFromFN(fp32ExpWidth, fp32SigWidth, a)\n    add.io.b := recFNFromFN(fp32ExpWidth, fp32SigWidth, b)\n    add.io.roundingMode := consts.round_near_even\n    add.io.detectTininess := consts.tininess_afterRounding\n    fNFromRecFN(fp32ExpWidth, fp32SigWidth, add.io.out)\n  }\n\n  def subFp32(a: UInt, b: UInt): UInt = {\n    val sub = Module(new AddRecFN(fp32ExpWidth, fp32SigWidth))\n    sub.io.subOp := true.B\n    sub.io.a := recFNFromFN(fp32ExpWidth, fp32SigWidth, a)\n    sub.io.b := recFNFromFN(fp32ExpWidth, fp32SigWidth, b)\n    sub.io.roundingMode := consts.round_near_even\n    sub.io.detectTininess := consts.tininess_afterRounding\n    fNFromRecFN(fp32ExpWidth, fp32SigWidth, sub.io.out)\n  }\n\n  def mulFp32(a: UInt, b: UInt): UInt = {\n    val mul = Module(new MulRecFN(fp32ExpWidth, fp32SigWidth))\n    mul.io.a := recFNFromFN(fp32ExpWidth, fp32SigWidth, a)\n    mul.io.b := recFNFromFN(fp32ExpWidth, fp32SigWidth, b)\n    mul.io.roundingMode := consts.round_near_even\n    mul.io.detectTininess := consts.tininess_afterRounding\n    fNFromRecFN(fp32ExpWidth, fp32SigWidth, mul.io.out)\n  }\n\n  def lessThanFp32(a: UInt, b: UInt): Bool = {\n    val compare = Module(new CompareRecFN(fp32ExpWidth, fp32SigWidth))\n    compare.io.a := recFNFromFN(fp32ExpWidth, fp32SigWidth, a)\n    compare.io.b := recFNFromFN(fp32ExpWidth, fp32SigWidth, b)\n    compare.io.signaling := false.B\n    compare.io.lt\n  }\n\n  def sumFp32(values: Seq[UInt]): UInt = {\n    require(values.nonEmpty, \"sumFp32 requires at least one value\")\n    values.reduce((a, b) => addFp32(a, b))\n  }\n}\n\nfinal case class StreamSpec(dataBits: Int, addrBits: Int) {\n  require(dataBits > 0, \"dataBits must be positive\")\n  require(addrBits > 0, \"addrBits must be positive\")\n}\n\nclass StageConfig(seqBits: Int) extends Bundle {\n  val seqlen = UInt(seqBits.W)\n  val prefill = Bool()\n  val singleQuery = Bool()\n}\n\nclass StreamBeat(spec: StreamSpec) extends Bundle {\n  val data = UInt(spec.dataBits.W)\n  val st = Bool()\n  val addr = UInt(spec.addrBits.W)\n  val last = Bool()\n}\n\nclass WeightWrite(dataBits: Int, addrBits: Int) extends Bundle {\n  val addr = UInt(addrBits.W)\n  val data = UInt(dataBits.W)\n}\n\nclass Int8QuantPorts extends Bundle {\n  val outInvScale = Input(UInt(32.W))\n  val outZeroPoint = Input(SInt(8.W))\n}\n\nclass LinearScalePorts extends Bundle {\n  val outScale = Input(UInt(32.W))\n  val biasScale = Input(UInt(32.W))\n}\n",
      "path": "accagent/runs/spatialacc_qwen_agent_fast_run/generated/chisel/src/main/scala/spatialaccagent/templates/Common.scala",
      "sha256": "991168fe5c5941f7b193e9722505eb2836df321f0c8dd15a2977b0dce03d143c",
      "truncated": false
    },
    {
      "bytes": 1914,
      "content": "package spatialaccagent.templates\n\nimport chisel3._\nimport chisel3.util._\n\nfinal case class KVCacheParams(\n  kvHeads: Int,\n  headDim: Int,\n  seqLen: Int,\n  elemsPerBeat: Int = 2,\n  elemBits: Int = 8,\n  batchSize: Int = 16\n) {\n  require(kvHeads > 0, \"kvHeads must be positive\")\n  require(headDim % elemsPerBeat == 0, \"headDim must be divisible by elemsPerBeat\")\n  val headBeats: Int = headDim / elemsPerBeat\n  val depth: Int = batchSize * kvHeads * seqLen * headBeats\n  val beatBits: Int = elemsPerBeat * elemBits\n  val addrBits: Int = log2Ceil(depth max 2)\n}\n\nclass KVCache(p: KVCacheParams) extends Module {\n  val io = IO(new Bundle {\n    val write = Flipped(Decoupled(new StreamBeat(StreamSpec(p.beatBits, p.addrBits))))\n    val readAddr = Flipped(Decoupled(UInt(p.addrBits.W)))\n    val read = Decoupled(new StreamBeat(StreamSpec(p.beatBits, p.addrBits)))\n  })\n\n  val dataMem = Reg(Vec(p.depth, UInt(p.beatBits.W)))\n  val stMem = RegInit(VecInit(Seq.fill(p.depth)(false.B)))\n  val lastMem = RegInit(VecInit(Seq.fill(p.depth)(false.B)))\n  val readData = Reg(UInt(p.beatBits.W))\n  val readAddrReg = Reg(UInt(p.addrBits.W))\n  val readSt = RegInit(false.B)\n  val readLast = RegInit(false.B)\n  val readValid = RegInit(false.B)\n\n  io.write.ready := true.B\n  when(io.write.fire) {\n    dataMem(io.write.bits.addr) := io.write.bits.data\n    stMem(io.write.bits.addr) := io.write.bits.st\n    lastMem(io.write.bits.addr) := io.write.bits.last\n  }\n\n  io.readAddr.ready := !readValid || io.read.ready\n  when(io.readAddr.fire) {\n    readData := dataMem(io.readAddr.bits)\n    readAddrReg := io.readAddr.bits\n    readSt := stMem(io.readAddr.bits)\n    readLast := lastMem(io.readAddr.bits)\n    readValid := true.B\n  }\n  when(io.read.fire) {\n    readValid := false.B\n  }\n\n  io.read.valid := readValid\n  io.read.bits.data := readData\n  io.read.bits.st := readSt\n  io.read.bits.addr := readAddrReg\n  io.read.bits.last := readLast\n}\n",

      "content": "package spatialaccagent.templates\n\nimport chisel3._\nimport chisel3.util._\n\nfinal case class LinearParams(\n  inDim: Int,\n  outDim: Int,\n  inLanes: Int = 8,\n  outLanes: Int = 8,\n  elemBits: Int = 16,\n  accBits: Int = 32,\n  outputBits: Int = 16,\n  batchSize: Int = 16,\n  maxSeqLen: Int = 16,\n  hasBias: Boolean = true,\n  fusedActivation: String = \"none\"\n) {\n  require(inDim > 0 && outDim > 0, \"linear dimensions must be positive\")\n  require(inDim % inLanes == 0, \"inDim must be divisible by inLanes\")\n  require(outDim % outLanes == 0, \"outDim must be divisible by outLanes\")\n  require(elemBits == 16 || elemBits == 32, \"Linear inputs and weights must be IEEE FP16 or FP32\")\n  require(accBits == 32, \"Linear accumulation must use FP32\")\n  require(outputBits == 16 || outputBits == 32, \"Linear output must be IEEE FP16 or FP32\")\n  require(fusedActivation == \"none\" || fusedActivation == \"relu\", \"unsupported fused activation\")\n  val inBeats: Int = inDim / inLanes\n  val outBeats: Int = outDim / outLanes\n  val inputBeatBits: Int = inLanes * elemBits\n  val outputBeatBits: Int = outLanes * outputBits\n  val weightTileBits: Int = inLanes * outLanes * elemBits\n  val weightDepth: Int = inBeats * outBeats\n  val inputAddrBits: Int = log2Ceil(batchSize * inBeats max 2)\n  val outputAddrBits: Int = log2Ceil(batchSize * outBeats max 2)\n  val weightAddrBits: Int = log2Ceil(weightDepth max 2)\n  val biasBeatBits: Int = outLanes * accBits\n}\n\nclass Linear(p: LinearParams) extends Module {\n  val io = IO(new Bundle {\n    val start = Input(Bool())\n    val cfg = Input(new StageConfig(log2Ceil(p.maxSeqLen + 1 max 2)))\n    val cfgValid = Input(Bool())\n    val weight = Flipped(Decoupled(new WeightWrite(p.weightTileBits, p.weightAddrBits)))\n    val bias = Flipped(Decoupled(new WeightWrite(p.biasBeatBits, log2Ceil(p.outBeats max 2))))\n    val scale = new LinearScalePorts\n    val in = Flipped(Decoupled(new StreamBeat(StreamSpec(p.inputBeatBits, p.inputAddrBits))))\n    val out = Decoupled(new StreamBeat(StreamSpec(p.outputBeatBits, p.outputAddrBits)))\n  })\n\n  val inputMem = Reg(Vec(p.inBeats, UInt(p.inputBeatBits.W)))\n  val weightMem = Reg(Vec(p.weightDepth, UInt(p.weightTileBits.W)))\n  val biasMem = Reg(Vec(p.outBeats, UInt(p.biasBeatBits.W)))\n\n  val weightLoadCnt = RegInit(0.U(p.weightAddrBits.W))\n  val weightLoaded = RegInit(false.B)\n  io.weight.ready := !weightLoaded && io.weight.bits.addr === weightLoadCnt\n  when(io.weight.fire) {\n    weightMem(weightLoadCnt) := io.weight.bits.data\n    when(weightLoadCnt === (p.weightDepth - 1).U) {\n      weightLoaded := true.B\n      weightLoadCnt := 0.U\n    }.otherwise {\n      weightLoadCnt := weightLoadCnt + 1.U\n    }\n  }\n\n  private val biasAddrBits = log2Ceil(p.outBeats max 2)\n  val biasLoadCnt = RegInit(0.U(biasAddrBits.W))\n  val biasLoaded = RegInit((!p.hasBias).B)\n  if (p.hasBias) {\n    io.bias.ready := !biasLoaded && io.bias.bits.addr === biasLoadCnt\n    when(io.bias.fire) {\n      biasMem(biasLoadCnt) := io.bias.bits.data\n      when(biasLoadCnt === (p.outBeats - 1).U) {\n        biasLoaded := true.B\n        biasLoadCnt := 0.U\n      }.otherwise {\n        biasLoadCnt := biasLoadCnt + 1.U\n      }\n    }\n  } else {\n    io.bias.ready := false.B\n  }\n\n  val parametersLoaded = weightLoaded && biasLoaded\n  val sLoad :: sPrep :: sCompute :: sEmit :: Nil = Enum(4)\n  val state = RegInit(sLoad)\n  val inCnt = RegInit(0.U(log2Ceil(p.inBeats max 2).W))\n  val rowCnt = RegInit(0.U(log2Ceil(p.inBeats max 2).W))\n  val outCnt = RegInit(0.U(log2Ceil(p.outBeats max 2).W))\n  val tokenCnt = RegInit(0.U(log2Ceil(p.batchSize max 2).W))\n  val vectorStart = RegInit(false.B)\n  val acc = Reg(Vec(p.outLanes, UInt(32.W)))\n\n  when(io.start && parametersLoaded) {\n    state := sLoad\n    inCnt := 0.U\n    rowCnt := 0.U\n    outCnt := 0.U\n    tokenCnt := 0.U\n  }\n\n  io.in.ready := state === sLoad && parametersLoaded\n  when(io.in.fire) {\n    inputMem(inCnt) := io.in.bits.data\n    when(inCnt === 0.U) {\n      vectorStart := io.in.bits.st\n    }\n    when(inCnt === (p.inBeats - 1).U) {\n      inCnt := 0.U\n      outCnt := 0.U\n      state := sPrep\n    }.otherwise {\n      inCnt := inCnt + 1.U\n    }\n  }\n\n  val biasVec = biasMem(outCnt).asTypeOf(Vec(p.outLanes, UInt(32.W)))\n  when(state === sPrep) {\n    for (o <- 0 until p.outLanes) {\n      if (p.hasBias) {\n        acc(o) := biasVec(o)\n      } else {\n        acc(o) := IeeeMath.fp32Zero\n      }\n    }\n    rowCnt := 0.U\n    state := sCompute\n  }\n\n  val inVec = inputMem(rowCnt).asTypeOf(Vec(p.inLanes, UInt(p.elemBits.W)))\n  val weightAddr = (outCnt * p.inBeats.U + rowCnt)(p.weightAddrBits - 1, 0)\n  val weightTile = weightMem(weightAddr).asTypeOf(Vec(p.outLanes, Vec(p.inLanes, UInt(p.elemBits.W))))\n  val dotProducts = Wire(Vec(p.outLanes, UInt(32.W)))\n  for (o <- 0 until p.outLanes) {\n    val products = (0 until p.inLanes).map { i =>\n      val inputFp32 = IeeeMath.toFp32(inVec(i), p.elemBits)\n      val weightFp32 = IeeeMath.toFp32(weightTile(o)(i), p.elemBits)\n      IeeeMath.mulFp32(inputFp32, weightFp32)\n    }\n    dotProducts(o) := IeeeMath.sumFp32(products)\n  }\n\n  when(state === sCompute) {\n    for (o <- 0 until p.outLanes) {\n      acc(o) := IeeeMath.addFp32(acc(o), dotProducts(o))\n    }\n    when(rowCnt === (p.inBeats - 1).U) {\n      state := sEmit\n    }.otherwise {\n      rowCnt := rowCnt + 1.U\n    }\n  }\n\n  val outVec = Wire(Vec(p.outLanes, UInt(p.outputBits.W)))\n  for (o <- 0 until p.outLanes) {\n    val activated = if (p.fusedActivation == \"relu\") {\n      Mux(IeeeMath.lessThanFp32(acc(o), IeeeMath.fp32Zero), IeeeMath.fp32Zero, acc(o))\n    } else {\n      acc(o)\n    }\n    outVec(o) := IeeeMath.fromFp32(activated, p.outputBits)\n  }\n\n  io.out.valid := state === sEmit\n  io.out.bits.data := outVec.asUInt\n  io.out.bits.st := vectorStart && outCnt === 0.U\n  io.out.bits.addr := tokenCnt * p.outBeats.U + outCnt\n  io.out.bits.last := outCnt === (p.outBeats - 1).U\n\n  when(io.out.fire) {\n    when(outCnt === (p.outBeats - 1).U) {\n      state := sLoad\n      outCnt := 0.U\n      tokenCnt := Mux(tokenCnt === (p.batchSize - 1).U, 0.U, tokenCnt + 1.U)\n    }.otherwise {\n      outCnt := outCnt + 1.U\n      state := sPrep\n    }\n  }\n}\n\nclass ParametricLinearInt8ToInt8(p: LinearParams) extends Linear(p)\n\nclass ParametricLinearInt8ToFP32(p: LinearParams)\n    extends Linear(p.copy(outputBits = 32))\n",

      "content": "package spatialaccagent.templates\n\nimport chisel3._\nimport chisel3.util._\n\nfinal case class AttentionMaskParams(\n  seqLen: Int,\n  causal: Boolean = true,\n  slidingWindow: Int = 0,\n  globalEvery: Int = 0\n) {\n  require(seqLen > 0, \"seqLen must be positive\")\n  require(slidingWindow >= 0, \"slidingWindow must be non-negative\")\n  require(globalEvery >= 0, \"globalEvery must be non-negative\")\n  val seqBits: Int = log2Ceil(seqLen + 1 max 2)\n  val rowBits: Int = log2Ceil(seqLen max 2)\n}\n\nclass AttentionMask(p: AttentionMaskParams) extends Module {\n  val io = IO(new Bundle {\n    val cfg = Input(new StageConfig(p.seqBits))\n    val cfgValid = Input(Bool())\n    val rowIndex = Input(UInt(p.rowBits.W))\n    val mask = Output(UInt(p.seqLen.W))\n  })\n\n  val seqLimit = RegInit(p.seqLen.U(p.seqBits.W))\n  when(io.cfgValid) {\n    seqLimit := Mux(io.cfg.seqlen === 0.U || io.cfg.seqlen > p.seqLen.U, p.seqLen.U, io.cfg.seqlen)\n  }\n\n  val bits = Wire(Vec(p.seqLen, Bool()))\n  val globalRow =\n    if (p.globalEvery > 0) io.rowIndex % p.globalEvery.U === 0.U else false.B\n  for (i <- 0 until p.seqLen) {\n    val idx = i.U(p.rowBits.W)\n    val active = i.U < seqLimit\n    val causalKeep = if (p.causal) idx <= io.rowIndex else true.B\n    val slidingKeep =\n      if (p.slidingWindow > 0) (idx + p.slidingWindow.U) > io.rowIndex else true.B\n    bits(i) := active && causalKeep && (slidingKeep || globalRow)\n  }\n  io.mask := bits.asUInt\n}\n",

Int,\n  lanes: Int = 12,\n  elemBits: Int = 8,\n  qkvElemsPerBeat: Int = 2,\n  batchSize: Int = 16,\n  maxSeqLen: Int = 16\n) {\n  require(qHeads % kvHeads == 0, \"qHeads must be divisible by kvHeads\")\n  require(headDim % qkvElemsPerBeat == 0, \"headDim must be divisible by qkvElemsPerBeat\")\n  val qDim: Int = qHeads * headDim\n  val kDim: Int = kvHeads * headDim\n  val vDim: Int = kvHeads * headDim\n  val outDim: Int = qDim + kDim + vDim\n  val linear = LinearParams(hiddenSize, outDim, inLanes = lanes, outLanes = lanes, elemBits = elemBits, outputBits = elemBits, batchSize = batchSize, maxSeqLen = maxSeqLen, hasBias = true)\n  val headBeats: Int = headDim / qkvElemsPerBeat\n  val qkvBeatBits: Int = 3 * qkvElemsPerBeat * elemBits\n  val headBits: Int = log2Ceil(qHeads max 2)\n  val addrBits: Int = log2Ceil(batchSize * headBeats max 2)\n  val kvGroupSize: Int = qHeads / kvHeads\n}\n\nclass QKVStreamBeat(p: QKVProjectionParams) extends Bundle {\n  val data = UInt(p.qkvBeatBits.W)\n  val st = Bool()\n  val head = UInt(p.headBits.W)\n  val addr = UInt(p.addrBits.W)\n  val last = Bool()\n}\n\nclass QKVProjection(p: QKVProjectionParams) extends Module {\n  val io = IO(new Bundle {\n    val start = Input(Bool())\n    val cfg = Input(new StageConfig(log2Ceil(p.maxSeqLen + 1 max 2)))\n    val cfgValid = Input(Bool())\n    val weight = Flipped(Decoupled(new WeightWrite(p.linear.weightTileBits, p.linear.weightAddrBits)))\n    val bias = Flipped(Decoupled(new WeightWrite(p.linear.biasBeatBits, log2Ceil(p.linear.outBeats max 2))))\n    val qScale = Input(UInt(32.W))\n    val kScale = Input(UInt(32.W))\n    val vScale = Input(UInt(32.W))\n    val in = Flipped(Decoupled(new StreamBeat(StreamSpec(p.linear.inputBeatBits, p.linear.inputAddrBits))))\n    val out = Decoupled(new QKVStreamBeat(p))\n  })\n\n  val linear = Module(new Linear(p.linear))\n  linear.io.start := io.start\n  linear.io.cfg := io.cfg\n  linear.io.cfgValid := io.cfgValid\n  linear.io.weight <> io.weight\n  linear.io.bias <> io.bias\n  linear.io.scale.outScale := io.qScale\n  linear.io.scale.biasScale := 0.U\n  linear.io.in <> io.in\n\n  val vecMem = Reg(Vec(p.linear.outBeats, UInt(p.linear.outputBeatBits.W)))\n  val collectCnt = RegInit(0.U(log2Ceil(p.linear.outBeats max 2).W))\n  val headCnt = RegInit(0.U(p.headBits.W))\n  val beatCnt = RegInit(0.U(log2Ceil(p.headBeats max 2).W))\n  val sCollect :: sEmit :: Nil = Enum(2)\n  val state = RegInit(sCollect)\n\n  linear.io.out.ready := state === sCollect\n  when(linear.io.out.fire) {\n    vecMem(collectCnt) := linear.io.out.bits.data\n    collectCnt := Mux(collectCnt === (p.linear.outBeats - 1).U, 0.U, collectCnt + 1.U)\n    when(linear.io.out.bits.last) {\n      headCnt := 0.U\n      beatCnt := 0.U\n      state := sEmit\n    }\n  }\n\n  val flat = Wire(Vec(p.outDim, UInt(p.elemBits.W)))\n  for (w <- 0 until p.linear.outBeats) {\n    val word = vecMem(w).asTypeOf(Vec(p.lanes, UInt(p.elemBits.W)))\n    for (l <- 0 until p.lanes) {\n      val idx = w * p.lanes + l\n      if (idx < p.outDim) {\n        flat(idx) := word(l)\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  val elemBase = beatCnt * p.qkvElemsPerBeat.U\n  val kvHead = headCnt / p.kvGroupSize.U\n  val qElems = Wire(Vec(p.qkvElemsPerBeat, UInt(p.elemBits.W)))\n  val kElems = Wire(Vec(p.qkvElemsPerBeat, UInt(p.elemBits.W)))\n  val vElems = Wire(Vec(p.qkvElemsPerBeat, UInt(p.elemBits.W)))\n  for (i <- 0 until p.qkvElemsPerBeat) {\n    val elem = elemBase + i.U\n    qElems(i) := flat(headCnt * p.headDim.U + elem)\n    kElems(i) := flat(p.qDim.U + kvHead * p.headDim.U + elem)\n    vElems(i) := flat((p.qDim + p.kDim).U + kvHead * p.headDim.U + elem)\n  }\n\n  io.out.valid := state === sEmit\n  io.out.bits.data := Cat(vElems.reverse ++ kElems.reverse ++ qElems.reverse)\n  io.out.bits.st := headCnt === 0.U && beatCnt === 0.U\n  io.out.bits.head := headCnt\n  io.out.bits.addr := beatCnt\n  io.out.bits.last := headCnt === (p.qHeads - 1).U && beatCnt === (p.headBeats - 1).U\n\n  when(io.out.fire) {\n    beatCnt := Mux(beatCnt === (p.headBeats - 1).U, 0.U, beatCnt + 1.U)\n    when(beatCnt === (p.headBeats - 1).U) {\n      headCnt := Mux(headCnt === (p.qHeads - 1).U, 0.U, headCnt + 1.U)\n    }\n    when(io.out.bits.last) {\n      state := sCollect\n    }\n  }\n}\n\nclass QKVProjector(p: QKVProjectionParams) extends QKVProjection(p)\n",

      "content": "package spatialaccagent.templates\n\nimport chisel3._\nimport chisel3.util._\n\nfinal case class RoPEParams(\n  qkv: QKVProjectionParams,\n  theta: Double = 10000.0,\n  localTheta: Option[Double] = None\n) {\n  require(qkv.qkvElemsPerBeat % 2 == 0, \"minimal RoPE template rotates even/odd element pairs\")\n  val positionBits: Int = log2Ceil(qkv.maxSeqLen max 2)\n}\n\nclass RoPE(p: RoPEParams) extends Module {\n  val io = IO(new Bundle {\n    val cfg = Input(new StageConfig(log2Ceil(p.qkv.maxSeqLen + 1 max 2)))\n    val cfgValid = Input(Bool())\n    val position = Input(UInt(p.positionBits.W))\n    val in = Flipped(Decoupled(new QKVStreamBeat(p.qkv)))\n    val out = Decoupled(new QKVStreamBeat(p.qkv))\n  })\n\n  io.in.ready := io.out.ready\n  io.out.valid := io.in.valid\n  io.out.bits.st := io.in.bits.st\n  io.out.bits.head := io.in.bits.head\n  io.out.bits.addr := io.in.bits.addr\n  io.out.bits.last := io.in.bits.last\n\n  val fields = io.in.bits.data.asTypeOf(Vec(3 * p.qkv.qkvElemsPerBeat, UInt(p.qkv.elemBits.W)))\n  val qOut = Wire(Vec(p.qkv.qkvElemsPerBeat, UInt(p.qkv.elemBits.W)))\n  val kOut = Wire(Vec(p.qkv.qkvElemsPerBeat, UInt(p.qkv.elemBits.W)))\n  val vOut = Wire(Vec(p.qkv.qkvElemsPerBeat, UInt(p.qkv.elemBits.W)))\n  val rotate = io.position(0)\n\n  for (i <- 0 until p.qkv.qkvElemsPerBeat) {\n    vOut(i) := fields(2 * p.qkv.qkvElemsPerBeat + i)\n  }\n  for (i <- 0 until p.qkv.qkvElemsPerBeat by 2) {\n    val qOdd = fields(i + 1).asSInt\n    val kOdd = fields(p.qkv.qkvElemsPerBeat + i + 1).asSInt\n    qOut(i) := Mux(rotate, AccMath.resizeSignedToUInt(-qOdd, p.qkv.elemBits), fields(i))\n    qOut(i + 1) := Mux(rotate, fields(i), fields(i + 1))\n    kOut(i) := Mux(rotate, AccMath.resizeSignedToUInt(-kOdd, p.qkv.elemBits), fields(p.qkv.qkvElemsPerBeat + i))\n    kOut(i + 1) := Mux(rotate, fields(p.qkv.qkvElemsPerBeat + i), fields(p.qkv.qkvElemsPerBeat + i + 1))\n  }\n\n  io.out.bits.data := Cat(vOut.reverse ++ kOut.reverse ++ qOut.reverse)\n}\n\nclass RoPEApply(p: RoPEParams) extends RoPE(p)\n",

      "content": "package spatialaccagent.templates\n\nimport chisel3._\nimport chisel3.util._\nimport chisel3.util.experimental.loadMemoryFromFileInline\nimport hardfloat._\nimport hardfloat.consts\n\nfinal case class SoftmaxParams(\n  seqLen: Int,\n  lanes: Int = 16,\n  elemBits: Int = 16,\n  fracBits: Int = 8,\n  batchSize: Int = 16,\n  causal: Boolean = true,\n  slidingWindow: Int = 0\n) {\n  require(seqLen > 0, \"seqLen must be positive\")\n  require(lanes > 0, \"lanes must be positive\")\n  require(elemBits == 16 || elemBits == 32, \"softmax elements must be IEEE fp16 or fp32\")\n  require(fracBits >= 0, \"fracBits must be non-negative\")\n  require(seqLen % lanes == 0, \"softmax requires seqLen divisible by lanes\")\n  require(slidingWindow >= 0, \"slidingWindow must be non-negative\")\n\n  val beats: Int = seqLen / lanes\n  val beatBits: Int = lanes * elemBits\n  val addrBits: Int = log2Ceil(batchSize * beats max 2)\n  val rowBits: Int = log2Ceil(seqLen max 2)\n  val seqBits: Int = log2Ceil(seqLen + 1 max 2)\n}\n\nclass Softmax(p: SoftmaxParams) extends Module {\n  val io = IO(new Bundle {\n    val cfg = Input(new StageConfig(p.seqBits))\n    val cfgValid = Input(Bool())\n    val rowIndex = Input(UInt(p.rowBits.W))\n    val mask = Input(UInt(p.seqLen.W))\n    val in = Flipped(Decoupled(new StreamBeat(StreamSpec(p.beatBits, p.addrBits))))\n    val out = Decoupled(new StreamBeat(StreamSpec(p.beatBits, p.addrBits)))\n  })\n\n  private val ExpTablePath =\n    \"src/main/resources/spatialaccagent/numeric/exp2_fraction_q24.memh\"\n  private val Fp32ExpWidth = 8\n  private val Fp32SigWidth = 24\n  private val Q11Width = 16\n  private val ExpFractionBits = 24\n  private val Log2eFixed = 2955\n\n  private def fp32ToSignedQ11(value: UInt): SInt = {\n    val scaled = IeeeMath.mulFp32(value, IeeeMath.fp32Constant(2048.0))\n    val convert = Module(new RecFNToIN(Fp32ExpWidth, Fp32SigWidth, Q11Width))\n    convert.io.in := recFNFromFN(Fp32ExpWidth, Fp32SigWidth, scaled)\n    convert.io.roundingMode := consts.round_near_even\n    convert.io.signedOut := true.B\n    convert.io.out.asSInt\n  }\n\n  private def unsignedQ24ToFp32(value: UInt): UInt = {\n    val convert = Module(new INToRecFN(value.getWidth, Fp32ExpWidth, Fp32SigWidth))\n    convert.io.signedIn := false.B\n    convert.io.in := value\n    convert.io.roundingMode := consts.round_near_even\n    convert.io.detectTininess := consts.tininess_afterRounding\n    val integerFp32 = Reg(Vec(p.beats, UInt(p.addrBits.W)))\n  val stMem = RegInit(VecInit(Seq.fill(p.beats)(false.B)))\n  val rowIndexReg = RegInit(0.U(p.rowBits.W))\n  val rowSeqLimitReg = RegInit(p.seqLen.U(p.seqBits.W))\n  val maskReg = RegInit(0.U(p.seqLen.W))\n\n  val sCollect :: sEmit :: Nil = Enum(2)\n  val state = RegInit(sCollect)\n  val collectCountBits = log2Ceil(p.beats max 2)\n  val emitCountBits = log2Ceil(p.beats max 2)\n  val emitBeatsBits = log2Ceil(p.beats + 1 max 2)\n  val collectCnt = RegInit(0.U(collectCountBits.W))\n  val emitCnt = RegInit(0.U(emitCountBits.W))\n  val emitBeats = RegInit(p.beats.U(emitBeatsBits.W))\n  val seqLimit = RegInit(p.seqLen.U(p.seqBits.W))\n\n  val normalizedCfgSeq = Mux(\n    io.cfg.seqlen === 0.U || = io.in.bits.data.asTypeOf(Vec(p.lanes, UInt(p.elemBits.W)))\n  val firstCollectBeat = collectCnt === 0.U\n  val rowForCollect = Mux(firstCollectBeat, io.rowIndex, rowIndexReg)\n  val seqForCollect = Mux(\n    firstCollectBeat,\n    Mux(io.cfgValid, normalizedCfgSeq, seqLimit),\n    rowSeqLimitReg\n  )\n  val maskForCollect = Mux(firstCollectBeat, io.mask, maskReg)\n\n  when(io.in.fire) {\n    when(firstCollectBeat) {\n      rowIndexReg := io.rowIndex\n      rowSeqLimitReg := Mux(io.cfgValid, normalizedCfgSeq, seqLimit)\n      maskReg := io.mask\n    }\n\n    addrMem(collectCnt) := io.in.bits.addr\n    stMem(collectCnt) := io.in.bits.st\n\n    for (i <- 0 until p.lanes) {\n      val index = collectCnt * p.lanes.U + i.U\n      val inActiveSequence = index < seqForCollect\n      val externalKeep = maskForCollect(index)\n      val causalKeep = if (p.causal) index <= rowForCollect else true.B\n      val slidingKeep =\n        if (p.slidingWindow > 0) {\n          index + p.slidingWindow.U > rowForCollect\n        } else {\n          true.B\n        }\n      val keep = inActiveSequence && externalKeep && causalKeep && slidingKeep\n      scoreMem(index) := inputVec(i)\n      keepMem(index) := keep\n    }\n\n    val finalConfiguredBeat = collectCnt === (p.beats - 1).U\n    when(io.in.bits.last || finalConfiguredBeat) {\n      emitBeats := collectCnt +& 1.U\n      emitCnt := 0.U\n      collectCnt := 0.U\n      state := sEmit\n    }.otherwise {\n      collectCnt := collectCnt + 1.U\n    }\n  }\n\n  val negativeMaximumFp32 = \"hff7fffff\".U(32.W)\n  val scoreFp32 = Wire(Vec(p.seqLen, UInt(32.W)))\n  val maxCandidates = Wire(Vec(p.seqLen, UInt(32.W)))\n  for (i <- 0 until p.seqLen) {\n    scoreFp32(i) := IeeeMath.toFp32(scoreMem(i), p.elemBits)\n    maxCandidates(i) := Mux(keepMem(i), scoreFp32(i), negativeMaximumFp32)\n  }\n\n  val maxScoreFp32 = (0 until p.seqLen)\n    .map(i => maxCandidates(i))\n    .reduce((a, b) => Mux(IeeeMath.lessThanFp32(a, b), b, a))\n\n  val expQ24 = Wire(Vec(p.seqLen, UInt(26.W)))\n  val minusSixteen = IeeeMath.fp32Constant(-16.0)\n  val expOneQ24 = (BigInt(1) << ExpFractionBits).U(26.W)\n\n  for (i <- 0 until p.seqLen) {\n    val shiftedFp32 = IeeeMath.subFp32(scoreFp32(i), maxScoreFp32)\n    val shiftedGeZero = !IeeeMath.lessThanFp32(shiftedFp32, IeeeMath.fp32Zero)\n    val shiftedLeMinusSixteen = !IeeeMath.lessThanFp32(minusSixteen, shiftedFp32)\n    val shiftedQ11 = fp32ToSignedQ11(shiftedFp32)\n    val logProductQ22 = shiftedQ11 * Log2eFixed.S(13.W)\n    val logProductBits = logProductQ22.asUInt\n    val fractionalAddress = logProductBits(21, 11)\n    val integerFloor = logProductQ22 >> 22\n    val shiftMagnitude = (0.S(logProductQ22.getWidth.W) - integerFloor).asUInt\n    val tableValue = expTable.read(fractionalAddress)\n    val rangeReducedExp = tableValue >> shiftMagnitude\n    val boundedExp = Mux(\n      shiftedGeZero,\n      expOneQ24,\n      Mux(shiftedLeMinusSixteen, 0.U(26.W), rangeReducedExp)\n    )\n    expQ24(i) := Mux(keepMem(i), boundedExp, 0.U)\n  }\n\n  val sumBits = 26 + log2Ceil(p.seqLen max 2)\n  val expSum = (0 until p.seqLen)\n    .map(i => expQ24(i).pad(sumBits))\n    .reduce((a, b) => a + b)\n  val safeExpSum = Mux(expSum === 0.U, 1.U(sumBits.W), expSum)\n\n  val probabilityBits = Wire(Vec(p.seqLen, UInt(p.elemBits.W)))\n  for (i <- 0 until p.seqLen) {\n    val scaledNumerator = Cat(expQ24(i), 0.U(ExpFractionBits.W))\n    val quotientWide = scaledNumerator / safeExpSum\n    val quotientQ24 = quotientWide(24, 0)\n    val remainder = scaledNumerator % safeExpSum\n    val doubledRemainder = remainder +& remainder\n    val extendedSum = Cat(0.U(1.W), safeExpSum)\n    val aboveHalf = doubledRemainder > extendedSum\n    val exactlyHalf = doubledRemainder === extendedSum\n    val roundUp = aboveHalf || (exactlyHalf && quotientQ24(0))\n    val roundedWide = quotientQ24 +& roundUp.asUInt\n    val roundedQ24 = roundedWide(24, 0)\n    val normalizedQ24 = Mux(expSum === 0.U, 0.U(25.W), roundedQ24)\n    val probabilityFp32 = unsignedQ24ToFp32(normalizedQ24)\n    probabilityBits(i) := IeeeMath.fromFp32(probabilityFp32, p.elemBits)\n  }\n\n  val outputVec = Wire(Vec(p.lanes, UInt(p.elemBits.W)))\n  for (i <- 0 until p.lanes) {\n    val index = emitCnt * p.lanes.U + i.U\n    outputVec(i) := probabilityBits(index)\n  }\n\n  io.out.valid := state === sEmit\n  io.out.bits.data := outputVec.asUInt\n  io.out.bits.st := stMem(emitCnt)\n  io.out.bits.addr := addrMem(emitCnt)\n  io.out.bits.last := emitCnt === (emitBeats - 1.U)\n\n  when(io.out.fire) {\n    when(io.out.bits.last) {\n      emitCnt := 0.U\n      state := sCollect\n      for (i <- 0 until p.seqLen) {\n        keepMem(i) := false.B\n      }\n    }.otherwise {\n      emitCnt := emitCnt + 1.U\n    }\n  }\n}\n",

      "content": "package QuantCommon\n\nimport hardfloat.consts\n\nobject Precision {\n  // \u57fa\u7840\u7cbe\u5ea6\u5b9a\u4e49\n  val INT8_WIDTH = 8\n  val UINT8_WIDTH = 8\n  val INT32_WIDTH = 32\n  val FP32_WIDTH = 32\n\n  // IEEE FP32 = 1(sign) + 8(exp) + 23(frac)\n  // HardFloat \u4e2d sigWidth \u5305\u542b\u9690\u85cf\u4f4d\uff0c\u56e0\u6b64\u53d6 24\n  val FP32_EXP_WIDTH = 8\n  val FP32_SIG_WIDTH = 24\n\n  // \u5f53\u524d\u52a0\u901f\u5668\u6bcf\u62cd\u5904\u7406 12 \u4e2a\u5143\u7d20\n  val LANES = 12\n  val SOFTMAX_LEN = 26\n\n  // \u5e38\u7528\u6253\u5305\u4f4d\u5bbd\n  val INT8_PACK_WIDTH = INT8_WIDTH * LANES\n  val FP32_PACK_WIDTH = FP32_WIDTH * LANES\n  val SOFTMAX_FP32_PACK_WIDTH = FP32_WIDTH * SOFTMAX_LEN\n\n  // \u91cf\u5316\u7ea6\u675f\n  val SCALE_WIDTH = FP32_WIDTH\n  val ZERO_POINT_WIDTH = UINT8_WIDTH\n\n  // HardFloat \u9ed8\u8ba4\u63a7\u5236\u53c2\u6570\n  val ROUNDING_MODE = consts.round_near_even\n  val DETECT_TININESS = consts.tininess_afterRounding\n}\n",

      "content": "package QuantCommon\n\nobject FpBackend {\n  sealed trait Mode\n  case object HardFloatCompat extends Mode\n  case object VivadoIp extends Mode\n\n  private var current: Mode = HardFloatCompat\n\n  def mode: Mode = current\n  def useVivadoIp: Boolean = current == VivadoIp\n\n  def setHardFloatCompat(): Unit = {\n    current = HardFloatCompat\n  }\n\n  def setVivadoIp(): Unit = {\n    current = VivadoIp\n  }\n}\n",

      "content": "package QuantCommon\n\nimport QuantCommon.Precision._\nimport chisel3._\nimport chisel3.experimental.ExtModule\nimport chisel3.util.{Cat, ShiftRegister}\nimport hardfloat._\n\nobject XilinxFpTargetConfig {\n  // Vivado 2021.1 floating_point IP, single precision, Speed_Optimized,\n  // Maximum_Latency=true, Flow_Control=Blocking\n  val AddLatency = 12\n  val MulLatency = 9\n  val DivLatency = 29\n  val SqrtLatency = 29\n  val CompareLatency = 3\n  val FixedToFloatLatency = 7\n  val FloatToFixedLatency = 7\n\n  // HardFloat DivSqrtRecFN_small uses sigWidth + 3 cycles for FP32.\n  val HardFloatDivSqrtLatency = FP32_SIG_WIDTH + 3\n  val DivSqrtPadLatency = DivLatency - HardFloatDivSqrtLatency\n}\n\nprivate object XilinxFpCompatUtil {\n  def keep[T <: Data](x: T): T = {\n    dontTouch(x)\n    x\n  }\n\n  def axisByteWidth(bits: Int): Int = ((bits + 7) / 8) * 8\n\n  def delayValid(in: Bool, stages: Int): Bool = {\n    if (stages == 0) in\n    else {\n      val regs = RegInit(VecInit(Seq.fill(stages)(false.B)))\n      regs(0) := in\n      for (i <- 1 until stages) {\n        regs(i) := regs(i - 1)\n      }\n      regs(stages - 1)\n    }\n  }\n}\n\nprivate object XilinxFpIpName {\n  def add(subOp: Boolean): String = if (subOp) \"fp_sub_sp_12\" else \"fp_add_sp_12\"\n  val mul = \"fp_mul_sp_9\"\n  def compare(kind: String): String = s\"fp_cmp_${kind}_sp_3\"\n  def intToFloat(intWidth: Int, signedIn: Boolean): String =\n    s\"fp_i2f_${if (signedIn) \"s\" else \"u\"}${intWidth}_sp_7\"\n  def floatToInt(intWidth: Int, signedOut: Boolean): String =\n    s\"fp_f2i_${if (signedOut) \"s\" else \"u\"}${intWidth}_sp_7\"\n  val div = \"fp_div_sp_29\"\n  val sqrt = \"fp_sqrt_sp_29\"\n}\n\nprivate class VivadoFpBinaryIp(moduleName: String, resultWidth: Int = FP32_WIDTH) extends ExtModule {\n  override def desiredName: String = moduleName\n  val aclk = IO(Input(Clock()))\n  val s_axis_a_tvalid = IO(Input(Bool()))\n  val s_axis_a_tready = IO(Output(Bool()))\n  val s_axis_a_tdata = IO(Input(UInt(FP32_WIDTH.W)))\n  val s_axis_b_tvalid = IO(Input(Bool()))\n  val s_axis_b_tready = IO(Output(Bool()))\n  val s_axis_b_tdata = IO(Input(UInt(FP32_WIDTH.W)))\n  val m_axis_result_tvalid = IO(Output(Bool()))\n  val m_axis_result_tready = IO(Input(Bool()))\n  val m_axis_result_tdata = IO(Output(UInt(resultWidth.W)))\n}\n\nprivate class VivadoFpBinaryIpNoResultReady(moduleName: String, resultWidth: Int = FP32_WIDTH) extends ExtModule {\n  override def desiredName: String = moduleName\n  val aclk = IO(Input(Clock()))\n  val s_axis_a_tvalid = IO(Input(Bool()))\n  val s_axis_a_tready = IO(Output(Bool()))\n  val s_axis_a_tdata = IO(Input(UInt(FP32_WIDTH.W)))\n  val s_axis_b_tvalid = IO(Input(Bool()))\n  val s_axis_b_tready = IO(Output(Bool()))\n  val s_axis_b_tdata = IO(Input(UInt(FP32_WIDTH.W)))\n  val m_axis_result_tvalid = IO(Output(Bool()))\n  val m_axis_result_tdata = IO(Output(UInt(resultWidth.W)))\n}\n\nprivate class VivadoFpUnaryIp(moduleName: String, inWidth: Int, resultWidth: Int) extends ExtModule {\n  override def desiredName: String = moduleName\n  val aclk = IO(Input(Clock()))\n  val s_axis_a_tvalid = IO(Input(Bool()))\n  val s_axis_a_tready = IO(Output(Bool()))\n  val s_axis_a_tdata = IO(Input(UInt(inWidth.W)))\n  val m_axis_result_tvalid = IO(Output(Bool()))\n  val m_axis_result_tready = IO(Input(Bool()))\n  val m_axis_result_tdata = IO(Output(UInt(resultWidth.W)))\n}\n\nprivate class VivadoFpUnaryIpNoResultReady(moduleName: String, inWidth: Int, resultWidth: Int) extends ExtModule {\n  override def desiredName: String = moduleName\n  val aclk = IO(Input(Clock()))\n  val m_axis_result_tdata = IO(Output(UInt(resultWidth.W)))\n}\n\nprivate class HardFloatAddCore(val subOpValue: Boolean) extends Module {\n  val io = IO(new Bundle {\n    val a = Input(UInt(FP32_WIDTH.W))\n    val b = Input(UInt(FP32_WIDTH.W))\n    val out = Output(UInt(FP32_WIDTH.W))\n  })\n\n  val add = Module(new AddRecFN(FP32_EXP_WIDTH, FP32_SIG_WIDTH))\n  add.io.subOp := subOpValue.B\n  add.io.a := recFNFromFN(FP32_EXP_WIDTH, FP32_SIG_WIDTH, io.a)\n  add.io.b := recFNFromFN(FP32_EXP_WIDTH, FP32_SIG_WIDTH, io.b)\n  add.io.roundingMode := ROUNDING_MODE\n  add.io.detectTininess := DETECT_TININESS.asBool\n  io.out := fNFromRecFN(FP32_EXP_WIDTH, FP32_SIG_WIDTH, add.io.out)\n}\n\nprivate class HardFloatMulCore extends Module {\n  val io = IO(new Bundle {\n    val a = Input(UInt(FP32_WIDTH.W))\n    val b = Input(UInt(FP32_WIDTH.W))\n    val out = Output(UInt(FP32_WIDTH.W))\n  })\n\n  val mul = Module(new MulRecFN(FP32_EXP_WIDTH, FP32_SIG_WIDTH))\n  mul.io.a := recFNFromFN(FP32_EXP_WIDTH, FP32_SIG_WIDTH, io.a)\n  mul.io.b := recFNFromFN(FP32_EXP_WIDTH, FP32_SIG_WIDTH, io.b)\n  mul.io.roundingMode := ROUNDING_MODE\n  mul.io.detectTininess := DETECT_TININESS.asBool\n  io.out := fNFromRecFN(FP32_EXP_WIDTH, FP32_SIG_WIDTH, mul.io.out)\n}\n\nprivate class HardFloatCompareCore extends Module {\n  val io = IO(new Bundle {\n    val a = Input(UInt(FP32_WIDTH.W))\n    val b = Input(UInt(FP32_WIDTH.W))\n    val lt = Output(Bool())\n    val eq = Output(Bool())\n    val gt = Output(Bool())\n  })\n\n  val cmp = Module(new CompareRecFN(FP32_EXP_WIDTH, FP32_SIG_WIDTH))\n  cmp.io.a := recFNFromFN(FP32_EXP_WIDTH, FP32_SIG_WIDTH, io.a)\n  cmp.io.b := recFNFromFN(FP32_EXP_WIDTH, FP32_SIG_WIDTH, io.b)\n  cmp.io.signaling := false.B\n  io.lt := cmp.io.lt\n  io.eq := cmp.io.eq\n  io.gt := cmp.io.gt\n}\n\nprivate class HardFloatIntToFpCore(val intWidth: Int, val signedIn: Boolean) extends Module {\n  val io = IO(new Bundle {\n    val in = Input(UInt(intWidth.W))\n    val out = Output(UInt(FP32_WIDTH.W))\n  })\n\n  val cvt = Module(new INToRecFN(intWidth, FP32_EXP_WIDTH, FP32_SIG_WIDTH))\n  cvt.io.signedIn := signedIn.B\n  cvt.io.in := io.in\n  cvt.io.roundingMode := ROUNDING_MODE\n  cvt.io.detectTininess := DETECT_TININESS\n  io.out := fNFromRecFN(FP32_EXP_WIDTH, FP32_SIG_WIDTH, cvt.io.out)\n}\n\nprivate class HardFloatFpToIntCore(val intWidth: Int, val signedOut: Boolean) extends Module {\n  val io = IO(new Bundle {\n    val in = Input(UInt(FP32_WIDTH.W))\n    val out = Output(UInt(intWidth.W))\n  })\n\n  val cvt = Module(new RecFNToIN(FP32_EXP_WIDTH, FP32_SIG_WIDTH, intWidth))\n  cvt.io.in := recFNFromFN(FP32_EXP_WIDTH, FP32_SIG_WIDTH, io.in)\n  cvt.io.roundingMode := ROUNDING_MODE\n  cvt.io.signedOut := signedOut.B\n  io.out := cvt.io.out\n}\n\nprivate class HardFloatDivSqrtCore(val sqrtOpValue: Boolean) extends Module {\n  val io = IO(new Bundle {\n    val inValid = Input(Bool())\n    val inReady = Output(Bool())\n    val a = Input(UInt(FP32_WIDTH.W))\n    val b = Input(UInt(FP32_WIDTH.W))\n    val outValid = Output(Bool())\n    val out = Output(UInt(FP32_WIDTH.W))\n  })\n\n  val divSqrt = Module(new DivSqrtRecFN_small(FP32_EXP_WIDTH, FP32_SIG_WIDTH, 0))\n  divSqrt.io.inValid := io.inValid\n  divSqrt.io.sqrtOp := sqrtOpValue.B\n  divSqrt.io.a := recFNFromFN(FP32_EXP_WIDTH, FP32_SIG_WIDTH, io.a)\n  divSqrt.io.b := recFNFromFN(FP32_EXP_WIDTH, FP32_SIG_WIDTH, io.b)\n  divSqrt.io.roundingMode := ROUNDING_MODE\n  divSqrt.io.detectTininess := DETECT_TININESS\n  io.inReady := divSqrt.io.inReady\n  io.outValid := Mux(sqrtOpValue.B, divSqrt.io.outValid_sqrt, divSqrt.io.outValid_div)\n  io.out := fNFromRecFN(FP32_EXP_WIDTH, FP32_SIG_WIDTH, divSqrt.io.out)\n}\n\nclass XilinxFpAddCompat(val subOp: Boolean = false) extends Module {\n  val aclk = XilinxFpCompatUtil.keep(IO(Input(Clock())))\n  val s_axis_a_tvalid = XilinxFpCompatUtil.keep(IO(Input(Bool())))\n  val s_axis_a_tready = XilinxFpCompatUtil.keep(IO(Output(Bool())))\n  val s_axis_a_tdata = XilinxFpCompatUtil.keep(IO(Input(UInt(FP32_WIDTH.W))))\n  val s_axis_b_tvalid = XilinxFpCompatUtil.keep(IO(Input(Bool())))\n  val s_axis_b_tready = XilinxFpCompatUtil.keep(IO(Output(Bool())))\n  val s_axis_b_tdata = XilinxFpCompatUtil.keep(IO(Input(UInt(FP32_WIDTH.W))))\n  val m_axis_result_tvalid = XilinxFpCompatUtil.keep(IO(Output(Bool())))\n  val m_axis_result_tready = XilinxFpCompatUtil.keep(IO(Input(Bool())))\n  val m_axis_result_tdata = XilinxFpCompatUtil.keep(IO(Output(UInt(FP32_WIDTH.W))))\n\n  if (FpBackend.useVivadoIp) {\n    val ip = Module(new VivadoFpBinaryIpNoResultReady(XilinxFpIpName.add(subOp)))\n    ip.aclk := aclk\n    ip.s_axis_a_tvalid := s_axis_a_tvalid\n    ip.s_axis_a_tdata := s_axis_a_tdata\n    ip.s_axis_b_tvalid := s_axis_b_tvalid\n    ip.s_axis_b_tdata := s_axis_b_tdata\n    s_axis_a_tready := ip.s_axis_a_tready\n    s_axis_b_tready := ip.s_axis_b_tready\n    m_axis_result_tvalid := ip.m_axis_result_tvalid\n    m_axis_result_tdata := ip.m_axis_result_tdata\n  } else {\n    withClockAndReset(aclk, reset) {\n      val core = Module(new HardFloatAddCore(subOp))\n      val inFire = s_axis_a_tvalid && s_axis_b_tvalid\n      core.io.a := s_axis_a_tdata\n      core.io.b := s_axis_b_tdata\n      s_axis_a_tready := true.B\n      s_axis_b_tready := true.B\n      m_axis_result_tvalid := XilinxFpCompatUtil.delayValid(inFire, XilinxFpTargetConfig.AddLatency)\n      m_axis_result_tdata := ShiftRegister(core.io.out, XilinxFpTargetConfig.AddLatency)\n    }\n  }\n}\n\nclass XilinxFpMulCompat extends Module {\n  val aclk = XilinxFpCompatUtil.keep(IO(Input(Clock())))\n  val s_axis_a_tvalid = XilinxFpCompatUtil.keep(IO(Input(Bool())))\n  val s_axis_a_tready = XilinxFpCompatUtil.keep(IO(Output(Bool())))\n  val s_axis_a_tdata = XilinxFpCompatUtil.keep(IO(Input(UInt(FP32_WIDTH.W))))\n  val s_axis_b_tvalid = XilinxFpCompatUtil.keep(IO(Input(Bool())))\n  val s_axis_b_tready = XilinxFpCompatUtil.keep(IO(Output(Bool())))\n  val s_axis_b_tdata = XilinxFpCompatUtil.keep(IO(Input(UInt(FP32_WIDTH.W))))\n  val m_axis_result_tvalid = XilinxFpCompatUtil.keep(IO(Output(Bool())))\n  val m_axis_result_tready = XilinxFpCompatUtil.keep(IO(Input(Bool())))\n  val m_axis_result_tdata = XilinxFpCompatUtil.keep(IO(Output(UInt(FP32_WIDTH.W))))\n\n  if (FpBackend.useVivadoIp) {\n    val ip = Module(new VivadoFpBinaryIpNoResultReady(XilinxFpIpName.mul))\n    ip.aclk := aclk\n    ip.s_axis_a_tvalid := s_axis_a_tvalid\n    ip.s_axis_a_tdata := s_axis_a_tdata\n    ip.s_axis_b_tvalid := s_axis_b_tvalid\n    ip.s_axis_b_tdata := s_axis_b_tdata\n    s_axis_a_tready :=  sqrt.m_axis_result_tready := true.B\n\n  io.inReady := Mux(io.sqrtOp, sqrt.s_axis_a_tready, div.s_axis_a_tready && div.s_axis_b_tready)\n  io.outValidDiv := div.m_axis_result_tvalid\n  io.outValidSqrt := sqrt.m_axis_result_tvalid\n  io.out := Mux(sqrt.m_axis_result_tvalid, sqrt.m_axis_result_tdata, div.m_axis_result_tdata)\n  io.exceptionFlags := 0.U\n}\n\nclass Int8ToFp32 extends SIntToFp32(INT8_WIDTH)\nclass Int8ToFp32Comb extends SIntToFp32Comb(INT8_WIDTH)\nclass UInt8ToFp32 extends UIntToFp32(UINT8_WIDTH)\nclass Int32ToFp32 extends SIntToFp32(INT32_WIDTH)\nclass Int32ToFp32Comb extends SIntToFp32Comb(INT32_WIDTH)\nclass Fp32ToInt8 extends Fp32ToSInt(INT8_WIDTH)\nclass Fp32ToUInt8 extends Fp32ToUInt(UINT8_WIDTH)\n",

      "content": "\n/*============================================================================\n\nThis Chisel source file is part of a pre-release version of the HardFloat IEEE\nFloating-Point Arithmetic Package, by John R. IN NO EVENT SHALL THE REGENTS OR CONTRIBUTORS BE LIABLE FOR ANY\nDIRECT, INDIRECT, INCIDENTAL, SPECIAL, EXEMPLARY, OR CONSEQUENTIAL DAMAGES\n(INCLUDING, BUT NOT LIMITED TO, PROCUREMENT OF SUBSTITUTE GOODS OR SERVICES;\nLOSS OF USE, DATA, OR PROFITS; OR BUSINESS INTERRUPTION) HOWEVER CAUSED AND\nON ANY THEORY OF LIABILITY, WHETHER IN CONTRACT, STRICT LIABILITY, OR TORT\n(INCLUDING NEGLIGENCE OR OTHERWISE) ARISING IN ANY WAY OUT OF THE USE OF THIS\nSOFTWARE, EVEN IF ADVISED OF THE POSSIBILITY OF SUCH DAMAGE.\n\n=============================================================================*/\n\npackage hardfloat\n\nimport chisel3._\nimport consts._\n\nclass\n    RecFNToRecFN(\n        inExpWidth: Int, inSigWidth: Int, outExpWidth: Int, outSigWidth: Int)\n    extends chisel3.RawModule\n{\n    val io = IO(new Bundle {\n        val in = Input(Bits((inExpWidth + inSigWidth + 1).W))\n        val roundingMode   = Input(UInt(3.W))\n        val detectTininess = Input(UInt(1.W))\n        val out = Output(Bits((outExpWidth + outSigWidth + 1).W))\n        val exceptionFlags = Output(Bits(5.W))\n    })\n\n    //------------------------------------------------------------------------\n    //------------------------------------------------------------------------\n    val rawIn = rawFloatFromRecFN(inExpWidth, inSigWidth, io.in);\n\n    if ((inExpWidth == outExpWidth) && (inSigWidth <= outSigWidth)) {\n\n        //--------------------------------------------------------------------\n        //--------------------------------------------------------------------\n        io.out            := io.in<<(outSigWidth - inSigWidth)\n        io.exceptionFlags := isSigNaNRawFloat(rawIn) ## 0.U(4.W)\n\n    } else {\n\n        //--------------------------------------------------------------------\n        //--------------------------------------------------------------------\n        val roundAnyRawFNToRecFN =\n            Module(\n                new RoundAnyRawFNToRecFN(\n                        inExpWidth,\n                        inSigWidth,\n                        outExpWidth,\n                        outSigWidth,\n                        flRoundOpt_sigMSBitAlwaysZero\n                    ))\n        roundAnyRawFNToRecFN.io.invalidExc     := isSigNaNRawFloat(rawIn)\n        roundAnyRawFNToRecFN.io.infiniteExc    := false.B\n        roundAnyRawFNToRecFN.io.in             := rawIn\n        roundAnyRawFNToRecFN.io.roundingMode   := io.roundingMode\n        roundAnyRawFNToRecFN.io.detectTininess := io.detectTininess\n        io.out            := roundAnyRawFNToRecFN.io.out\n        io.exceptionFlags := roundAnyRawFNToRecFN.io.exceptionFlags\n    }\n}\n\n",

      "content": "\n/*============================================================================\n\nThis Chisel source file is part of a pre-release version of the HardFloat IEEE\nFloating-Point Arithmetic Package, by John R. Hauser (with some contributions\nfrom Yunsup Lee and Andrew Waterman, mainly concerning testing).\n\nCopyright 2010, 2011, 2012, 2013, 2014, 2015, 2016 The Regents of the\nUniversity of California. Redistributions in binary form must reproduce the above copyright notice,\n    this list of conditions, and the following disclaimer in the documentation\n    and/or other materials provided with the distribution.\n\n 3. Neither the name of the University nor the names of its contributors may\n    be used to endorse or promote products derived from this software without\n    specific prior written permission.\n\nTHIS SOFTWARE IS PROVIDED BY THE REGENTS AND CONTRIBUTORS \"AS IS\", AND ANY\nEXPRESS OR IMPLIED WARRANTIES, INCLUDING, BUT NOT LIMITED TO, THE IMPLIED\nWARRANTIES OF MERCHANTABILITY AND FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE, ARE\nDISCLAIMED.  IN NO EVENT SHALL THE REGENTS OR CONTRIBUTORS BE LIABLE FOR ANY\nDIRECT, INDIRECT, INCIDENTAL, SPECIAL, EXEMPLARY, OR CONSEQUENTIAL DAMAGES\n(INCLUDING, BUT NOT LIMITED TO, PROCUREMENT OF SUBSTITUTE GOODS OR SERVICES;\nLOSS OF USE, DATA, OR PROFITS; OR BUSINESS INTERRUPTION) HOWEVER CAUSED AND\nON ANY THEORY OF LIABILITY, WHETHER IN CONTRACT, STRICT LIABILITY, OR TORT\n(INCLUDING NEGLIGENCE OR OTHERWISE) ARISING IN ANY WAY OUT OF THE USE OF THIS\nSOFTWARE, EVEN IF ADVISED OF THE POSSIBILITY OF SUCH DAMAGE.\n\n=============================================================================*/\n\npackage hardfloat\n\nimport chisel3._\n\nobject recFNFromFN\n{\n    def apply(expWidth: Int, sigWidth: Int, in: Bits) =\n    {\n        val rawIn = rawFloatFromFN(expWidth, sigWidth, in)\n        rawIn.sign ##\n          (Mux(rawIn.isZero, 0.U(3.W), rawIn.sExp(expWidth, expWidth - 2)) |\n                Mux(rawIn.isNaN, 1.U, 0.U)) ##\n            rawIn.sExp(expWidth - 3, 0) ##\n            rawIn.sig(sigWidth - 2, 0)\n    }\n}\n\n",

      "content": "\n/*============================================================================\n\nThis Chisel source file is part of a pre-release version of the HardFloat IEEE\nFloating-Point Arithmetic Package, by John R. All rights reserved.\n\nRedistribution and use in source and binary forms, with or without\nmodification, are permitted provided that the following conditions are met:\n\n 1. Redistributions in binary form must reproduce the above copyright notice,\n    this list of conditions, and the following disclaimer in the documentation\n    and/or other materials provided with the distribution.\n\n 3. Neither the name of the University nor the names of its contributors may\n    be used to endorse or promote products derived from this software without\n    specific prior written permission.\n\nTHIS SOFTWARE IS PROVIDED BY THE REGENTS AND CONTRIBUTORS \"AS IS\", AND ANY\nEXPRESS OR IMPLIED WARRANTIES, INCLUDING, BUT NOT LIMITED TO, THE IMPLIED\nWARRANTIES OF MERCHANTABILITY AND FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR PURPOSE, ARE\nDISCLAIMED.  IN NO EVENT SHALL THE REGENTS OR CONTRIBUTORS BE LIABLE FOR ANY\nDIRECT, INDIRECT, INCIDENTAL, SPECIAL, EXEMPLARY, OR CONSEQUENTIAL DAMAGES\n(INCLUDING, BUT NOT LIMITED TO, PROCUREMENT OF SUBSTITUTE GOODS OR SERVICES;\nLOSS OF USE, DATA, OR PROFITS; OR BUSINESS INTERRUPTION) HOWEVER CAUSED AND\nON ANY THEORY OF LIABILITY, WHETHER IN CONTRACT, STRICT LIABILITY, OR TORT\n(INCLUDING NEGLIGENCE OR OTHERWISE) ARISING IN ANY WAY OUT OF THE USE OF THIS\nSOFTWARE, EVEN IF ADVISED OF THE POSSIBILITY OF SUCH DAMAGE.\n\n=============================================================================*/\n\npackage hardfloat\n\nimport chisel3._\nimport chisel3.util._\n\nobject fNFromRecFN\n{\n    def apply(expWidth: Int, sigWidth: Int, in: Bits) =\n    {\n        val minNormExp = (BigInt(1)<<(expWidth - 1)) + 2\n\n        val rawIn = rawFloatFromRecFN(expWidth, sigWidth, in)\n\n        val isSubnormal = rawIn.sExp < minNormExp.S\n        val denormShiftDist = 1.U - rawIn.sExp(log2Up(sigWidth - 1) - 1, 0)\n        val denormFract = ((rawIn.sig>>1)>>denormShiftDist)(sigWidth - 2, 0)\n\n        val expOut =\n            Mux(isSubnormal,\n                0.U,\n                rawIn.sExp(expWidth - 1, 0) -\n                  ((BigInt(1)<<(expWidth - 1)) + 1).U\n            ) | Fill(expWidth, rawIn.isNaN || rawIn.isInf)\n        val fractOut =\n            Mux(isSubnormal,\n                denormFract,\n                Mux(rawIn.isInf, 0.U, rawIn.sig(sigWidth - 2, 0))\n            )\n        Cat(rawIn.sign, expOut, fractOut)\n    }\n}\n\n",
      "path": "accagent/runs/spatialacc_qwen_agent_fast_run/generated/chisel/src/main/scala/hardfloat/fNFromRecFN.scala",
